# State of Trade & Trade Goods on Krator (5.1 Multimodal Network — Rebaked Geology)

Generated by [gemini_trade_model.py](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/tools/scale-model/gemini_trade_model.py) from [geology-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/kits/resources/data/geology-gemini.json) and the native 5.1 road network [net51-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/openworld/net51-gemini.json) (`claude/ancient-network-9cc3e7`) into [trade-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/openworld/trade-gemini.json) on branch `gemini-trade` (leaving all base files untouched).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Model Summary**: **111 trade goods** (including `ship-timber` & `ship-hulls`, Opal Valley `photonic-opals`, Tiguantin `cushion-wool` & separate `wormwick`, East Abyss `abyss-lac`, and exterior pelagic `frost-coral` & `ria-amber`) solved across **53 polities, maritime poleis & abstract exterior regions** (`175` settlements & gateways) over a **four-regime three-layer directed 4 km Dijkstra multimodal graph** (`Layer 0` Overland + `Layer 1` Sea/Lake + `Layer 2` Directed Navigable Rivers & Eternian Canals) backed by the rebaked 5.1 geology, `135` reconciled regions ([world-pins-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/openworld/world-pins-gemini.json)), `claude/ancient-network-9cc3e7` ([net51-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/openworld/net51-gemini.json)), and `98` supplemental polity highways & stateless dirt roads ([roads-supplement-gemini.json](file:///C:/Users/travi/Documents/krator-trade/openworld/roads-supplement-gemini.json)).
> - **120,000 Metropolitan Economic Scale (`Iziz` & `Fulcrum`) + Exterior Sea Gateways (`The Crook` & `Ria Valley`)**: `Iziz` and `Fulcrum` are modeled at their full **120,000** metropolitan economic demand & supply (rendered smaller in-game for playability). Abstract exterior regions **`The Crook`** (`frost-coral`) and **`Ria Valley`** (`ria-amber`) arrive exclusively by ship into **`Farport`** and draw **`gold`, `silver`, and Krator's unique regional specialties** back out through Farport.
> - **Directed River & Lake Barges (`Jimjam -> Palewharf` Alabaster, `Shim` River Outlet), Surface `Arcbeam–Parabola` Highway, Intra-Xanadu Roads & `Catch/Twins` Stops**: `Layer 2` & calm `inland_lake` `Layer 1` model bulk river and lake barges (`Jimjam -> Palewharf` `alabaster` across Jimjam's Lake, `Shim` `Riv 0`, `Port Tobin` `Riv 12`, `Farport`, `Reed`, `Ostrukh-on-Water`, `Dapor`), **mid-quality Eternal State highways (`0.72x`)** linking `Palewharf`, `Bastion`, `Sheritu`, `Mirabilis`, `Veil`, `Tone`, `Lux`, and `Farport` to `Fulcrum`, pure **surface Ancient highway** between `Arcbeam` and `Parabola` (zero ancient tubes), **intra-Xanadu trade on surface roads** (zero ancient routes), explicit stop-by-stop trade along **`The Catch` & `The Twins`** (`Parabola -> Bab al-Hawa -> Kolli Oasis -> Arfwai Oasis -> Catch Salt Works -> Stair -> The Twins / White Gills -> Strongbow`), and the northern **`Qoold Lheek ↔ Leng ↔ Jimjam` dirt road** exiting exclusively down the eastern highway (zero Eternian transit, zero bulk log transport).
> - **Shipbuilding & Naval Stores Chain + Gunpowder & Steel Triangle + Opal Valley Three Grades**: Shipbuilding splits crooked knees/spars (`ship-timber`) and caulking/rigging (`pitch-tar`, `cordage`, `cotton` canvas) from commissioned hulls (`ship-hulls`, built at `Nephtos` for the League, `Kautoa` for the `Beast Riders`, `Almua`/`Shim`, `Port Mira`, `Farport`, `Shell`). No military power is self-sufficient in gunpowder (`coal` + volcanic `sulfur` + arid playa / *Da-Holwey* guano `saltpeter`). `Opal Valley` exports both court `gems` (`black`/`fire` opals) and `photonic-opals` (`Yusar` cybernetics, `Roketstad` optics, `Yuni` Geomancers).
> - **Glass Chain, East Abyss Rubber Scale-Tree, Fermented/Distilled Luxury Spirits, Vale Opium & Finished Crafts/Arms**: `silica-sand` + Catch/kelp `alkali` + `coal`/`peat` feed glasshouses (`glass` windows, bulbs & spirit bottles); East Abyss Carboniferous **`rubber scale-trees`** (`Sfenops`, `Liikofi`, `Locus`, `Mungo`, `Reed`) yield sulfur-vulcanized `rubber`; fermented & botanical chains convert grain -> `beer`/`whiskey` (`+ peat` -> Iron Republic `scotch`), rice/`salt-rice` -> `sake`, Eternian grapes -> `wine` (`+ sugar` -> `champagne`), cane -> `rum` (`+ isle-spice` -> Throne `ranjrum`), cultivated agave -> `tequila` vs wild Dasri agave -> `mezcal` (led by `Jabal`), inner-crater `tobacco` -> `cigars`, and Xanadu sky-poppy -> `opium`, alongside finished `clothes`, `furniture`, `firearms`, `armor`, and melee `weapons`.
> - **Ring Sea Smuggling, 50-km `Port Mira` Truce Zone & Fleetless Peoples**: **`Port Mira`** (`Hookfleet`) is both a major **Ranj (`isle-spice`)** supplier (alongside **`Ybera`**, `West Ring Islanders` (`Taikoro` & `Kautono`), `Zey'danin`/`Temora`, and `Andaru`) and a **pirate + smuggler haven** accepting all ships under a strict **50-km no-piracy zone**. **`Nephtos`** (`League: Tethys`) is a **smuggling (never piracy) haven** whose contested island claim makes **`Kautoa`** hostile to `Tethys` (`0.08x`) and the `League` (`0.28x`). **`Marr`** remains friendly to smugglers as their preferred Izani entrepôt. **`Zeijani`**, **`Andaru`**, **`Tiguantin`**, and **`Beast Riders`** have **zero fleets** (`Dhelv` is strictly an inland Throne cavern — maritime traders must come to neighboring anchorages or overland trails to trade with them).

## 4. Complete Good-by-Good Atlas (All 111 Goods)

### I. Metals, Compass Magnets, Bullion & Structural/Alloy Salvage

#### `iron` — Iron & Steel (`bulk`, unit: `t`)
- **Role**: mined iron ore & smelted bar; heavy bulk cargo feeding weapons, armor, firearms & tools
- **Cross-Polity Volume**: **`966.01 t/yr`** · **Pirate Capture**: `20.988 t` · **Mode Split**: Ship `20.3%` | River/Canal `8.0%` | Highway `59.6%` | Caravan `9.2%` | Tunnel/Pass `2.9%`
- **Producers (Exporters)**: **Eternal State** (`71.05` exp / `31586.56` surp, 33.1%), **Yuni Emirate** (`97.43` exp / `8953.93` surp, 9.4%), **Iron Republic** (`24.5` exp / `8822.33` surp, 9.2%), **Vothic Kingdom** (`43.85` exp / `8767.49` surp, 9.2%), **Empire of Iziz** (`87.29` exp / `6812.4` surp, 7.1%), **Talovolci Chiefs** (`34.54` exp / `5683.86` surp, 5.9%)
- **Importers (Customers)**: **Tabisti: Liikofi** (`263.06` imp / `263.06` need, 27.0%), **Tabisti: Sfenops** (`263.06` imp / `263.06` need, 27.0%), **League: Ys** (`135.57` imp / `134.79` need, 13.8%), **Silkhead (Korona Gateway)** (`67.85` imp / `67.85` need, 7.0%), **Hookfleet** (`59.94` imp / `61.62` need, 6.3%), **League: Trigon** (`42.95` imp / `42.61` need, 4.4%)

| Flow (`Origin -> Destination`) | Origin Polity -> Buyer Polity | Volume | Dist (`eff km`) | Mode | Ports, Passes & Corridors Traversed |
|:---|:---|---:|---:|:---|:---|
| **Mungo -> Liikofi** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Liikofi | `133.154 t` | 95 km (`128.1`) | `highway` | Desert Nomad Bandits |
| **Reed -> Sfenops** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Sfenops | `106.549 t` | 28 km (`33.4`) | `highway` | direct trail |
| **Verge -> Liikofi** | Yuni Emirate -> Tabisti: Liikofi | `94.177 t` | 379 km (`617.7`) | `highway` | Desert Nomad Bandits |
| **Reed upriver fields (to be named) -> Sfenops** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Sfenops | `89.044 t` | 59 km (`79.5`) | `highway` | Desert Nomad Bandits |
| **Furrow -> Sfenops** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Sfenops | `61.681 t` | 53 km (`65.8`) | `highway` | Desert Nomad Bandits |
| **Port Mira -> Ys** | Hookfleet -> League: Ys | `2.938 t` | 558 km (`133.0`) | `ship` | PIRATE PRIZE FENCE; ports: Port Mira -> Ys |

- **Cross-Polity Volume**: **`638.01 t/yr`** · **Pirate Capture**: `37.254 t` · **Mode Split**: Ship `26.2%` | River/Canal `13.2%` | Highway `46.4%` | Caravan `8.0%` | Tunnel/Pass `6.2%`

#### `bronze` — Bronze (`medium`, unit: `t`)
- **Role**: cast fittings, bells, naval cannon, armor/weapon mounts & Eternian window tracery
- **Cross-Polity Volume**: **`198.71 t/yr`** · **Pirate Capture**: `6.558 t` · **Mode Split**: Ship `15.4%` | River/Canal `26.6%` | Highway `31.6%` | Caravan `9.5%` | Tunnel/Pass `16.9%`
